/**
 * ============================================================================
 * PULSE Engine v2.3 (Security Hardened + Base64 Ready)
 * Author: Icehellionx
 * Role: Subtext / Emotion CNN classifier for RP chatbots.
 * ============================================================================
 */

(function () {
    'use strict';

    // ============================================================================
    // CONFIG & SAFETY
    // ============================================================================
    var DEBUG = 0;
    var MAX_TOKENS = 60;
    var MAX_LAYERS = 10; // Safety limit for loops

    function safeLog(msg) {
        try { if (console && console.log) console.log(msg); } catch (e) { }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // MODEL LOADER
    // ============================================================================
    var MODEL = null;
    var INIT_ATTEMPTED = false;

    function b64Decode(str) {
        try {
            // Standard Base64 decode for ASCII JSON
            return atob(str);
        } catch (e) {
            // Fallback for Unicode strings if needed later
            return decodeURIComponent(atob(str).split('').map(function (c) {
                return '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2);
            }).join(''));
        }
    }

    function initModel() {
        if (MODEL || INIT_ATTEMPTED) return;
        INIT_ATTEMPTED = true;

        var raw = "";
        try { raw = getRawModelData(); } catch (e) { return; }

        if (!raw || raw.length < 50) {
            // No data loaded, silent return
            return;
        }

        var parsed;
        try {
            // Detect if Base64 (starts with '{' is JSON, else Base64)
            if (raw.trim().charAt(0) !== '{') {
                raw = b64Decode(raw);
            }
            parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
            raw = null; // Garbage collect huge string immediately
        } catch (e) {
            safeLog("[Pulse] Parse Error: " + e.message);
            return;
        }

        try {
            var w = parsed.weights || {};
            var s = parsed.scales || {};

            // Dequantize (INT8 -> Float32)
            function deq(vals, scale) {
                if (!vals || scale == null) return null;
                var len = vals.length;
                var out = new Float32Array(len);
                for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
                    out[i] = vals[i] * scale;
                }
                return out;
            }

            // --- Dynamic Layer Loading (Fixed: No infinite loop) ---
            var convs = [];
            for (var i = 0; i < MAX_LAYERS; i++) {
                var wKey = "c" + i + "w";
                var bKey = "c" + i + "b";

                if (!w[wKey]) break; // Natural stop

                // Default kernel sizes if not specified [3, 4, 5, 6...]
                var kSize = (i < 4) ? (i + 3) : 3;

                convs.push({
                    w: deq(w[wKey], s[wKey]),
                    b: deq(w[bKey], s[bKey]),
                    k: kSize
                });
            }

            // Build Vocab
            var vocabMap = {};
            if (Array.isArray(parsed.vocab)) {
                for (var v = 0; v < parsed.vocab.length; v++) {
                    vocabMap[parsed.vocab[v]] = v;
                }
            }

            // Heads
            var head1W = w.emo_w || w.rel_w;
            var head2W = w.sub_w || w.pow_w;

            MODEL = {
                vocabMap: vocabMap,
                unkId: (vocabMap["<unk>"] !== undefined) ? vocabMap["<unk>"] : 1,
                embedDim: parsed.embed_dim || 32,
                convs: convs,
                embedding: deq(w.emb, s.emb),
                h1: {
                    W: deq(head1W, s.emo_w || s.rel_w),
                    B: deq(w.emo_b || w.rel_b, s.emo_b || s.rel_b),
                    labels: parsed.emotion_labels || [],
                    name: parsed.emotion_labels ? "Emotion" : "Relation",
                    type: 'softmax'
                },
                h2: {
                    W: deq(head2W, s.sub_w || s.pow_w),
                    B: deq(w.sub_b || w.pow_b, s.sub_b || s.pow_b),
                    labels: parsed.subtext_labels || [],
                    name: parsed.subtext_labels ? "Subtext" : "Power",
                    type: !!w.sub_w ? 'sigmoid' : 'softmax'
                }
            };

        } catch (e) {
            safeLog("[Pulse] Init logic failed: " + e.message);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // INFERENCE ENGINE
    // ============================================================================
    function cnn_tokenize(text) {
        return (text || "")
            .toLowerCase()
            .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s\[\]]/g, " ")
            .trim()
            .split(/\s+/)
            .filter(Boolean)
            .slice(0, MAX_TOKENS);
    }

    function conv1d_max_relu(inputCHW, weight, bias, C, L, k) {
        if (!weight || !bias) return new Float32Array(0);
        var O = (weight.length / (C * k)) | 0;
        var out = new Float32Array(O);
        var pad = (k - 1) >> 1;

        for (var f = 0; f < O; f++) {
            var maxVal = -999999.0;
            var wOff = f * C * k;
            var bVal = bias[f];

            for (var t = 0; t < L; t++) {
                var s = bVal;
                for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                    var baseInput = c * L;
                    var baseWeight = wOff + c * k;
                    for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) {
                        var idx = t + j - pad;
                        if (idx >= 0 && idx < L) {
                            s += inputCHW[baseInput + idx] * weight[baseWeight + j];
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (s < 0) s = 0; // ReLU
                if (s > maxVal) maxVal = s;
            }
            out[f] = maxVal;
        }
        return out;
    }

    function run_inference(text) {
        initModel();
        if (!MODEL) return null;

        var tokens = cnn_tokenize(text);
        var ids = new Int32Array(MAX_TOKENS);
        for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            var t = tokens[i];
            ids[i] = (MODEL.vocabMap[t] !== undefined) ? MODEL.vocabMap[t] : MODEL.unkId;
        }

        var C = MODEL.embedDim;
        var T = MAX_TOKENS;
        var embedded = new Float32Array(C * T);

        for (var t = 0; t < T; t++) {
            var id = ids[t];
            var off = id * C;
            for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                embedded[c * T + t] = MODEL.embedding[off + c];
            }
        }

        var feats = [];
        var total_feat = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < MODEL.convs.length; i++) {
            var cv = MODEL.convs[i];
            var res = conv1d_max_relu(embedded, cv.w, cv.b, C, T, cv.k);
            feats.push(res);
            total_feat += res.length;
        }

        var flat = new Float32Array(total_feat);
        var ptr = 0;
        for (var k = 0; k < feats.length; k++) {
            flat.set(feats[k], ptr);
            ptr += feats[k].length;
        }

        function computeHead(head) {
            var W = head.W;
            var B = head.B;
            var N = head.labels.length;
            var logits = new Float32Array(N);

            for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) {
                var sum = B[i];
                var wOff = i * total_feat;
                for (var j = 0; j < total_feat; j++) {
                    sum += W[wOff + j] * flat[j];
                }
                logits[i] = sum;
            }

            var probs = new Float32Array(N);
            if (head.type === 'sigmoid') {
                for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) probs[i] = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.exp(-logits[i]));
            } else {
                var maxL = -999999.0;
                for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) if (logits[i] > maxL) maxL = logits[i];
                var sumExp = 0.0;
                for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) {
                    probs[i] = Math.exp(logits[i] - maxL);
                    sumExp += probs[i];
                }
                for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) probs[i] /= sumExp;
            }
            return probs;
        }

        return {
            h1: computeHead(MODEL.h1),
            h2: computeHead(MODEL.h2)
        };
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // MAIN EXECUTION
    // ============================================================================
    // Safe guard: check if context is accessible before proceeding
    if (typeof context === "undefined") return;

    context.character = context.character || {};
    context.chat = context.chat || {};

    // Ensure we don't crash on empty chat
    var msgs = context.chat.last_messages || [];
    var last = (msgs.length > 0) ? msgs[msgs.length - 1].message : "";

    if (last && last.length > 1) {
        try {
            var result = run_inference(last);
            if (result && MODEL) {

                var fmt = function (labels, probs) {
                    var out = [];
                    for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) out.push({ l: labels[i], p: probs[i] });
                    out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.p - a.p });
                    return out.slice(0, 2).map(function (o) {
                        return o.l + ":" + Math.round(o.p * 100) + "%";
                    }).join(", ");
                };

                var s1 = fmt(MODEL.h1.labels, result.h1);
                var s2 = fmt(MODEL.h2.labels, result.h2);

                var line = "[PULSE]: " + MODEL.h1.name + ": " + s1 + " | " + MODEL.h2.name + ": " + s2 + "[/PULSE]";

                if (DEBUG) safeLog(line);

                // Append output
                context.character.scenario = (context.character.scenario || "") + "\n" + line;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            safeLog("[Pulse Error]: " + e.message);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // RAW MODEL DATA
    // ============================================================================
    function getRawModelData() {
        return [
            // Insert data chunks here
            '{"model_type":"cnn_subtext_emotion_int8_v1","vocab":["<pad>","<unk>","i","m","feel","to","a","this","the","you","if","it","t","not","right","for","now","just","of","is","and","in","honestly","being","like","my","really","kinda","even","that","she","s","he","at","real","don","though","but","can","no","little","me","feeling","on","with","gonna","lie","be","point","re","out","they","joke","super","when","sort","mean","haha","about","lol","good","weirdly","sec","say","your","lowkey","tbh","feels","how","up","so","know","her","way","what","we","too","honest","while","want","keep","hurt","fine","his","more","low","their","let","as","says","than","edge","have","nervous","moment","everything","them","down","okay","mood","are","won","time","stop","something","here","get","over","from","hard","talk","trying","myself","excited","whatever","back","safe","going","completely","into","before","do","every","there","am","anxious","happy","doesn","pissed","worn","hyped","all","go","saying","didn","again","frustrated","annoyed","keeps","makes","away","kind","tired","eyes","need","off","voice","still","pumped","comfortable","else","then","might","sure","best","see","ready","laughs","clear","bored","much","make","one","shouldn","energy","god","someone","sad","sounds","swear","think","explain","care","loud","between","anything","admit","quiet","nothing","because","ll","small","room","b","serious","us","straight","second","by","done","why","ve","hear","told","believe","without","wish","should","look","pretend","making","oh","hate","stay","words","instead","once","asks","through","around","mind","move","actually","d","or","enough","chest","everyone","head","next","alright","looks","absolutely","heart","together","waiting","truth","speaking","under","until","did","could","things","hands","already","fun","reason","blunt","only","table","word","glad","an","thing","some","telling","whole","today","never","has","sound","sense","happens","start","matter","tight","wow","upfront","own","clearly",
